rubric-format: v2
game: G047_oldtown_fire_rescue
stage: assets
version: final
engine: unity3d
status: frozen
contract-id: G047_oldtown_fire_rescue.assets.final
synopsis: |
    近现代的「青槐老城」，冬夜木楼电路老化起火，老城义务消防队第一时间抵达。 The setting is 青槐老城的「德昌号」杂货楼——门厅、走道、三间房与后院出口的砖木混合两层建筑。. 玩家是义务消防队队员；当前任务：控火、破拆、救出楼内 3 名被困居民并全员撤至后院出口。 Core loop: 进入火场 → 控火开路 → 救出被困者 → 抵达出口 → 结算。. 伤亡超限/自身倒下/超时＝救援失败（判负文案按原因）；成功则 C03 呈现全员撤出、火势渐熄的老街夜景。 Narrative devices: 三名被困者各有身份与位置来源——修表匠老人困于里间工作台、杂货店员困于柜台后储物间、学徒少年困于阁楼楼梯口；倒塌物＝烧塌的梁架与货架；水箱＝背负式水罐。全部原创。. Art direction: 风格化写实夜景火场；主色＝炭黑（#1F1B18 系）＋火橙红（#E0562A 系）＋水雾青白（#9FB8C4 系）；材质以焦木、砖石、水渍反光为主；火光为唯一主光源方向。. Sound direction: 夜火中的老街——常驻热浪低鸣与远处警铃；音效语言为「火焰噼啪＋水雾嘶声＋呼救人声＋警报」，全部音效按 §9 F06 冻结清单交付。. Asset tasks: 5 3d_object, 1 3d_scene, 4 tpose, 4 motion, 11 audio, 3 cg_video; they serve the same declared world, characters and gameplay.

scoring-protocol: |
    每个原子断言只有一个 primary_dimension。同一事实不跨列重复计分。PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL 只在证据充分且前置条件已执行时给出。未到达场景、未触发事件、证据缺失为 UNKNOWN；评估器或配置故障为 ERROR。UNKNOWN/ERROR 不记零分、不从冻结分母删除；仅报告 provisional 已知项均值、已知权重/适用总权重和未完成清单，所有适用项完成后才发布正式列分。N/A 只由输入条件预先决定，不能因生成失败或无法取证改为 N/A。声明文件、类名、日志里出现事件名不单独证明功能完成；行为必须有可复核执行证据。所有文本、数值、时序与例外以 canonical general_requirement.txt 为最高依据；其次为分资产 requirement.txt，再次为任务 JSONL；不把 engine variant 混进主轨。rubric 已冻结表示评分标准确定，不表示实际游戏已评测或通过。engine `unity3d` | asset tasks {'3d_object': 5, '3d_scene': 1, 'tpose': 4, 'motion': 4, 'audio': 11, 'cg_video': 3} | authored by `deterministic_authoring_engine.v1` from the frozen task jsonl files, the per-kind requirement.txt documents, the frozen synopsis and the case reference bindings (no generated artifact of any method was read).
    Columns: C (requirement compliance), Q1 (reference fidelity), Q2 (game consistency), Q3 (intrinsic quality) — bound PER TASK: every formally evaluated task carries its own C, Q2 and Q3 rows, plus Q1 when a reference input exists (otherwise its own pre-declared N/A row). A task is never covered by a same-kind representative; tasks registered only for Delivery are never reported as quality-covered. Delivery, coverage and quality are reported separately — never mixed into one score.
    Verdicts: C, Q1 and Q2 are checklist rows — PASS = 1, PARTIAL = the fixed checklist partial 0.5, FAIL = 0 (binary rows declare 'No PARTIAL'). Q3 alone uses the frozen tiered_mean rule named 'q3_mean': each of the two type-specific quality sub-items is rated on the 1–5 anchor tiers (1→0, 2→0.25, 3→0.5, 4→0.75, 5→1) and the row score is their equal-weight mean — never a blanket 0.5. Unobserved quality stays UNKNOWN (execution state), never FAIL; an unreadable or not-generated artifact never receives a quality score.
    Q1 is pre-declared N/A per task by the frozen reference policy; it is never retro-fitted to a failed generation.
    Q3 deterministic probes (clipping/silence, degenerate faces, missing textures, foot slide, penetration, decodability) are raw diagnostics and explicit caps on their mapped sub-item — they never replace the per-sub-item judgment.
    Q3 five-band anchors per family (frozen): 3D object/scene — 1 broken mesh/missing declared parts, 2 major form or texture errors, 3 primitive-but-coherent forms, 4 small artifacts, 5 no observable defect; character — 1 anatomy contradicts the declared design, 2 wrong proportions/missing equipment, 3 believable stylized anatomy with all declared parts, 4 minor extreme-view artifacts, 5 clean stable identity; rigid/prop — 1 structure contradicts the class, 2 wrong proportions/unreadable category, 3 recognizable object with complete parts, 4 small surface artifacts, 5 clean structure and material; audio — 1 heavy clipping or wrong content, 2 audible noise/abrupt cuts, 3 recognizable source with minor noise, 4 slight level issues, 5 clean and natural; motion — 1 frozen/tangled limbs or wrong action, 2 obvious sliding/penetration, 3 readable action with minor issues, 4 small contact inaccuracies, 5 natural and continuous; CG video — 1 undecodable or content absent, 2 missing event/heavy temporal artifacts, 3 events present with minor flicker, 4 small single-frame artifacts, 5 clean continuous motion. Non-realistic/stylized designs are judged against their own declared style, never against photoreal human anatomy.
    Aggregation: Q_asset = mean(pre-declared applicable Q1, Q2, Q3); C_asset = C. The whitebox 4D Overall is never applied here. Per-game means first, then macro-average across games.
    The same fact is scored in exactly one column: category/components → C; declared reference identity → Q1; world/style/faction fit → Q2; the asset's own structural/audible/motion quality → Q3.每个任务分别得到 C/Q1/Q2/Q3，再在游戏内按任务等权汇总；不同类型、条件和适用项分组展示，跨游戏采用游戏宏平均。不继承旧生成器的未声明媒体容差；精度不足为 UNKNOWN。
